Let's design local optimizer in detail:

Function `fft_conv(a)`:
```
def _fft_dtype...
size=1<<((2*n-1)-1).bit_length()
fa=np.zeros(size); fa[:n]=a
fa=np.fft.rfft(fa)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:2*n-1]
c=np.maximum(c,0)
return c
```
Need ensure sum of a=1.

Function `corr_p_a(p,a)`:
```
size=1<<((2*n-1)-1).bit_length() # same as conv
fp=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
r=np.fft.irfft(fp*np.conj(fa), size)[:n]
# due small negative clip?
```
Check orientation with a simple example: n=2, a=[1,2], p at k=1 maybe? c conv [1,4,4]. p=[0,1,0]. r_i should ∑_k p_k a_{k-i}=a_{1-i} valid: i=0 -> a1=2, i=1 -> a0=1. FFT corr p,a m=0:∑a_j p_j =2; m=1:∑a_j p_{j+1}=a0 p1 +a1 p2=1. Good. irfft(P*conj(A)) gives [2,1]? likely.

Enhance with exact convolution if n small? FFT fine.

Softmax p:
```
def softmax_lag_weights(c, sharp):
    z = c - c.max()
    # scale by sharp: z *= sharp? If sharp = T/M, then z*(T/M) = (c-M)*T/M = (c-M)/temp where temp=M/sharp.
    # Instead use temp = M/sharp. z=(c-M)/temp = sharp*(c-M)/M.
    z = sharp * (c - M) / M
    z = np.clip(z, -50, 0)
    p=np.exp(z); p/=p.sum()
```
If sharp large, p concentrated. sharp can be 5-50. previous temp_frac -> sharp=1/temp_frac ~4.5 initially. We can use sharp schedule: 5 -> 30. But if c has many close values, p broad.

Multiplicative update step:
```
def step_softmax(a, p, best, sharp, max_small):
    r=corr_p_a(p,a)
    r = np.maximum? no, can be positive. s = r - dot(a,r)
    # scale
    scale = np.linalg.norm(s) or maxabs
    # try log-changes: -eta * s/scale
    for eta in ...:
       logw = -eta * s/scale
       # clip logw to [-5,5] to avoid extreme
       h = a * np.exp(logw - logw weighted? maybe normalize includes)
       h = h / h.sum()
       sc=2n*fft_conv(h).max()
       if sc < best: return h
```
But doing FFT conv for each eta (say 6) per iteration may be okay but many iterations. We can do a backtracking line search: start with a moderate eta, if no improvement try smaller. Use one improved step per iteration. To speed, use current c? Convolution changes; must recompute. For n=512 size1024, conv ~0.5ms? In Python/numpy maybe 1ms, thousands okay. 6*1000=6000 conv ~6s. Fine. For n=2048 size4096 maybe 5ms; still okay.

Parameter eta: direction s may have opposite sign. We want drop high r positions. If we choose logw = -eta * s/scale, eta positive reduces where s positive, increases where negative. But if global max lag active, this might increase some coefficients. Line search picks best.

However pure gradient direction may be too local; we can also use "top-k active" directives:
- p = uniform over top k lags of c (or weighted) sometimes.
- p = delta at argmax.
This can reduce a single peak strongly.

We want g on maybe positive but not normalized. The optimum may have sum large? We want a direction g with sum 1? The paper maximizes sum g then normalizes. The previous normalized g to sum1 before mixing (g/Sg), then tried t. But if Sg huge, g/Sg maybe small? Actually maximizing sum gives a feasible point with as much mass as possible under a*g≤M; normalizing maintains shape. Good.

`local_search(a, time_limit, rng, state, n_target=None, verbose)`:
```
a = normalize_nonneg(a)
n = a.size
deadline = time.monotonic()+time_limit
best = a.copy(); best_score=compute_score_fast(a)
update_global(state,best,best_score)
no_improve=0
sharp=8
stagnation=0
it=0
while time.monotonic()<deadline:
    it+=1
    c=fft_conv(a); M=c.max(); score=2*n*M
    if score < best_score: ... no_improve=0
    else no_improve+=1
    if no_improve > 50:
        # average of best and random perturb?
        a = random_perturb_imp(best, rng, 0.1)
        # maybe identify top lags density
        no_improve=0
        sharp=min(60, sharp*1.2)
        if stagnation>=5: break? no, try other configs
    if it % 100 ==0 and no_improve>20 and LP available and n<=2048:
        a_lp = lp_move(a,c,M,rng)
        if a_lp and score(a_lp)<best_score: a=a_lp
    # choose p
    if rng.random()<0.15:
       # one-hot top few
       k=int(rng.integers(1,5)); idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]; p=zeros; p[idx]=1/k
    else:
       p=softmax(c, sharp)
    # gradient move
    h = multiplicative_move(a,p, best_score)
    if h is not None: a=h; # maybe sharp = max(2, sharp*0.95)
    else:
       # smaller steps or random
       if no_improve % 10 ==0: a = random_perturb(a,rng,0.05)
       # adapt sharp
       if no_improve>10: sharp=min(60, sharp*1.1)
       else: sharp=max(2, sharp*0.98)
```
But `multiplicative_move` itself computes corr and tries updates. It also returns the score of new a to avoid recompute? We can return (h, sc). We can pass current score.

Multiplicative move details:
```
def multiplicative_move(a, p, current_score):
    r=corr_p_a(p,a.size)
    # numerical small neg set 0? r can be positive; due FFT small negative maybe clip to 0? Since a,p nonneg, r nonnegative mathematically; clip.
    r = np.maximum(r,0)
    s = r - float(np.dot(a,r))
    # direction: reduce positive s. Use zero mean already.
    s_abs = float(np.max(np.abs(s)))
    if s_abs < 1e-15: return None
    # Try eta correspond to log changes. Since logw = eta * s/s_abs. s positive -> reduce if eta negative. Use -eta.
    # We can sample log_change values: 0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.5
    best_h=None; best_sc=current_score
    for eta in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.5):
       logw = -eta * s/s_abs
       # for stability, center mean in log? not necessary after normalize; but to avoid overflow clip
       logw = np.clip(logw, -8.0, 8.0)
       h = a * np.exp(logw)
       hs = h.sum()
       if hs<=0: continue
       h /= hs
       sc = score_from_conv(fft_conv(h)) # maybe cache
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_h=h
    return best_h,best_sc
```
But if s has both positive/negative, `s/s_abs` max 1. logw for negative up to +eta. If eta=1.5, some values increase by exp1.5=4.5. Fine.

Implement convolution with optional real FFT and small negative clipping.
```
def fft_conv(a):
    n=a.size
    size=1
    need=2*n-1
    while size<need: size <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:need]
    # remove small negative
    c[c<0]=0
    return c
```
`np.fft.rfft(a, size)` pads automatically if size arg? Yes rfft(a, n=size) works. We can avoid creating zeros. Use `np.fft.rfft(a, size)`. Good.
`corr(p,a)`:
```
def corr_pa(p,a):
    n=a.size; need=2*n-1; size power2
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fp*np.conj(fa), size)[:n]
    r[r<0]=0
    return r
```
Check size enough for cross p length2n-1 and a n; need max index j+m ≤2n-2. L≥2n-1 enough.

Random perturb function:
```
def random_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.1):
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    if a.size==1: return a
    # multiplicative log noise
    noise = rng.normal(0, strength, size=a.size)
    # low-pass smooth noise to avoid jagged
    wlen=max(1, min(a.size, 1 + a.size//50))
    if wlen>1:
        w=np.ones(wlen)/wlen
        noise=np.convolve(noise,w,mode='same')
    h=a*np.exp(noise)
    # optional block move: choose interval, multiply by exp(block_strength)
    return normalize_nonneg(h)
```
Maybe block jumps help escape. But multiplicative smooth enough.

We can also do **mass transfer between blocks**: choose two Block intervals and shift mass proportionally. But not necessary.

Local_search adaptive strategy:
```
def local_search_seq(a, time_limit, rng, state):
    a=normalize(a)
    n=a.size
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score_fast(a)
    update_global(state,best,best_score)
    no_improve=0
    sharp=10.0
    last_lp=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it +=1
       c=fft_conv(a); M=c.max(); cur=2*n*M
       if cur<best_score - 1e-12:
           best=a.copy(); best_score=cur; update_global
           no_improve=0
           # gradually focus
           sharp=max(4.0, sharp*0.985)
       else:
           no_improve +=1
       if no_improve >= 80:
           # restart from best with bigger perturbation, maybe vary sharp
           a = random_perturb(best, rng, 0.2)
           no_improve=0
           sharp=float(rng.uniform(5,40))
           continue
       # choose p
       if rng.random()<0.2:
           # top k onehot
           k=int(rng.integers(1,6))
           idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
           p=np.zeros_like(c); p[idx]=1.0/idx.size
       elif rng.random()<0.2:
           # inverse temp broad
           p=all_ones? (uniform over support?) maybe sqrt? Use p = c / c.sum()?? This gradient of sum? Uniform weights all lags; r is average? Might reduce sum not max. Not needed.
       else:
           p=softmax_lag_weights(c, sharp)
       # multiplicative move with line search
       moved, new_score, new_a = try_step(a,p,cur)
       if new_a is not None:
           a=new_a; cur=new_score
       else:
           # no improvement: occasionally try smaller/larger random direction
           if no_improve % 5==0:
              a = random_perturb(a, rng, 0.05)
           if no_improve > 20:
              sharp=min(80.0, sharp*1.1)
           # LP escape maybe
           if no_improve > 40 and (time.monotonic()-last_lp)>15 and n<=2048:
              last_lp=time.monotonic()
              lp_a = lp_move(a,c,M)
              if lp_a is not None:
                  # line search with a?
                  for t in ...: ...
```

`try_step` line search details:
```
def try_step(a, p, current_score):
    n=a.size
    r=corr_pa(p,a)
    # due FFT small negatives, but r can be zero where p none.
    mean_r=float(np.dot(a,r))
    s=r - mean_r
    s_max=float(np.max(np.abs(s)))
    if not finite or s_max<1e-15: return None,None
    # We can scale s to unit std instead of max to avoid rare large
    s = s / s_max
    best_h=None; best_sc=current_score
    # use candidate log changes, descending maybe.
    for eta in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        logw = -eta * s
        # center logw by weighted mean? Not necessary but improves normalize stability: logw -= dot(logw,a)
        logw = logw - float(np.dot(logw, a))
        logw = np.clip(logw, -10,10)
        h=a*np.exp(logw)
        hs=h.sum()
        if hs<=0 or not finite: continue
        h /= hs
        sc=score_fast(h)
        if sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_h=h
    return best_h,best_sc
```
Centering logw by weighted mean ensures multiplicative update has no first-order sum change? It may reduce variance and keep score stable. But does it alter direction? It removes common shift in log, which does not affect normalized a? For normalized h, multiplying all a by same factor cancels. So centering logw has no effect on h! Because exp(logw-c)=exp(logw)*e^{-c}; normalize cancels. So no need. But it can improve numerical if c large; we clip. Weighted mean maybe zero? We can skip.

Eta list: With s normalized max1, eta=4 gives log factor up to e^4=55 at negatives, too large may spike. Clip 6? Use eta up to 2. Since step small. For nonconvex, larger may jump basin but may hurt. Use (0.1,0.3,1.0,2.0). Could spend time.

Alternatively use adaptive based on `no_improve`: if stuck, try larger eta. In local_search pass `max_eta`.
`try_step(a,p,current_score, eta_list)`.

Could also try **opposite direction** with small eta as exploration? No.

But if `s` has max normalized, for broad p, s variation maybe small; eta max 2 may be too conservative. Could scale by `np.std(s)`: if max=1 but typical .1, eta=2 gives typical .2 log; okay. We can define `scale = max(np.std(s), 1e-12)` and use `s/scale`. But max normalization limits extremes. Maybe better:
```
scale = np.sqrt(np.mean(s*s))
if scale <1e-15 return
s_scaled = s/scale
eta_list = [0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0] # standard deviations
```
Then typical log change eta*1. If direction has outliers, clip. Use this.

`add_pool(pool, name, a)`:
```
def add_pool(pool, name, a):
    try:
       a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
       a=np.clip(a,0,None)
       s=float(a.sum())
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-15 or a.size<2: return
       a=a/s
       pool.append((name,a.copy()))
    except: pass
```

Let's prove correlation orientation with a simple test in code? Can't run, but reason. For p delta at k, `corr_pa` via `ifft(FFT(p)*conj(FFT(a)))[:n]`.
DFT conventions: numpy `ifft(X) = 1/n ∑ X_j e^{2πi jk/n}`. `fft(a)_j = ∑_m a_m e^{-2πi jm/n}`. Product = ∑ a_m e^{-...jm} * P_j. This is circular cross-correlation? Known: `ifft(fft(x)*conj(fft(y)))` gives cross-correlation `corr[k] = ∑_n x[n] y[n+k]` (circular). Yes. With p zero-padded, a zero-padded. `r[k]=∑_m a_m p[m+k mod L]`. For k=0..n-1, m max n-1, m+k max 2n-2 <L, so no mod. Good. In gradient, r_i = ∑_k p_k a_{k-i}. Let m=k-i, k=m+i, sum over m a_m p_{m+i}=r[i]. Good.

`softmax_lag_weights`:
```
def softmax_lag_weights(c, sharp):
    c=np.asarray(c)
    M=float(c.max())
    if M<=0: return uniform c size
    z=sharp*(c-M)/M
    z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    p=np.exp(z)
    s=p.sum()
    if s==0: p=c/M? maybe uniform top
    return p/s
```
Note if sharp=0 uniform? We use sharp>=4.

In local_search, if p from onehot top k, `p` sum1. For onehot at single max, direction s positions around that lag. Good.

Let's design `local_search` fully:
```
def local_search(a, time_limit, rng, state, name=''):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=a.size
    if n<2: return a
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0.0,float(time_limit))
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score_fast(a)
    update_global(state,best,best_score,f"init {name}")
    no_improve=0
    sharp=10.0
    last_lp=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    # maybe initial sharp based on n? no.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it+=1
        c=fft_conv(a)
        M=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        cur=2.0*n*M
        if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
            best=a.copy(); best_score=float(cur)
            update_global(state,best,best_score,f"improve {name} it={it}")
            no_improve=0
            sharp=max(4.0, sharp*0.99)
        else:
            no_improve +=1
            if no_improve > 10:
                 sharp=min(80.0, sharp*1.05)
        if no_improve >= 120:
            a=random_perturb(best, rng, 0.2)
            no_improve=0
            sharp=float(rng.uniform(5.0,40.0))
            continue
        # p selection
        if rng.random() < 0.25:
             k = int(rng.integers(1, 7)) # 1..6
             if k >= c.size: idx=np.arange(c.size)
             else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
             p=np.zeros_like(c); p[idx]=1.0/idx.size
        elif rng.random() < 0.15:
             # inverse of c? No. maybe all top 2k with power
             p=softmax_lag_weights(c, max(1.0, sharp*0.25))
        else:
             p=softmax_lag_weights(c, sharp)
        eta_list=(0.3,1.0,3.0)
        if no_improve>20:
             eta_list=(0.5,1.5,4.0,8.0)
        new_a,new_sc=try_step(a,p,cur,eta_list)
        if new_a is not None:
            a=new_a; cur=new_sc
        else:
            # no improvement: sometimes random micro perturb
            if no_improve % 7 == 0:
                a=random_perturb(a, rng, 0.05)
            # LP escape
            if no_improve >= 50 and n <= 1200 and (time.monotonic()-last_lp) > 10.0:
                 last_lp=time.monotonic()
                 g=lp_move(a,c,M)
                 if g is not None:
                     for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.8):
                         h=(1-t)*a + t*g
                         h=normalize_nonneg(h)
                         sc=score_fast(h)
                         if sc < cur:
                             a=h; cur=sc; break
    update_global(state,best,best_score,...)
    return best,best_score
```
But if `try_step` returns improvement, we set `a` but local best not updated until next loop; okay. It may spend many iterations without updating printed but fine.

Now `try_step`:
```
def try_step(a,p,current_score,eta_list):
    n=a.size
    r=corr_pa(p,a)
    # sometimes r may have tiny neg, clip
    r=np.maximum(r,0)
    mean_r=float(np.dot(a,r))
    s=r-mean_r
    scale=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(s*s)))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale<=1e-15: return None,None
    s=s/scale
    best_h=None; best_sc=current_score
    for eta in eta_list:
        logw=-eta*s
        # clip
        logw=logw - float(np.dot(logw,a)) # optional cancel common shift? Not affect normalized but if we don't normalize? It avoids overflow? We'll normalize anyway. Could keep for numerical to prevent exp large if global mean positive.
        logw=np.clip(logw,-6.0,6.0)
        h=a*np.exp(logw)
        hs=float(h.sum())
        if hs<=1e-300 or not np.isfinite(hs): continue
        h/=hs
        sc=score_fast(h)
        if sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_h=h
    return best_h,best_sc
```
Centering logw by weighted mean not change normalized h. It can make max logw smaller. Good. But due clipping after center, fine. We need ensure `np.dot(logw,a)` finite. yes.

`normalize_nonneg`: ensure finite. If all zeros return uniform? But we don't want inf. Implement:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=1e-300:
        return np.ones(max(1,a.size),dtype=np.float64)/max(1,a.size)
    return a/s
```

def update_global(state, seq, score, tag=''):
    if not np.isfinite(score): return False
    seq=normalize_nonneg(seq)
    if seq.size==0: return False
    old=state['score']
    if score < old - 1e-12:
        state['score']=float(score)
        state['seq']=seq.copy()
        if tag or not np.isfinite(old) or score < old - 0.005:
            print(f"update {tag} score={score:.12f} n={seq.size}", flush=True)
    return True
```

```
def run_phase(scored, total_time, rng, state):
    if total_time < 5: return
    tasks=[]
    props=[0.40,0.25,0.15,0.10,0.05,0.05]
    for i,(sc,name,a) in enumerate(scored[:len(props)]):
        limit=total_time*props[i]
        if limit>=2:
            tasks.append((a, limit, f"{name}_top{i}"))
    # if only one task, give all
    for a,limit,name in tasks:
       if time.monotonic() + 5 > deadline? local_search has limit but propose can check
       local_search(a, limit, rng, state, name)
```

`random_perturb` smooth noise:
```
def random_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.1):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return a
    log_noise=rng.normal(0.0, float(strength), size=n)
    wlen=max(1, min(n, 1 + max(1, n//100)))
    if wlen>1:
        w=np.ones(wlen)/wlen
        log_noise=np.convolve(log_noise,w,mode='same')
    h=a*np.exp(log_noise)
    # rare block multiply
    if n>4 and rng.random()<0.3:
       width=max(2, min(n//5, n))
       start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width)))
       h[start:start+width] *= np.exp(float(strength)*rng.normal(0.0,1.0))
    return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(h,0.0))
```
Note mode='same' for even wlen? okay.

```

`resize_to_length`: For upsample, linear interpolation may not preserve singular accurately. We can use `np.interp`.
```
def resize_to_length(a,N):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   n=a.size
   if n==N: return a
   if n==0: return np.ones(N)/N
   if N==1: return np.array([1.0])
   if n<N:
       x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n)
       x_new=np.linspace(0,1,N)
       out=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
   else:
       out=np.zeros(N)
       for i in range(N):
          lo=i*n//N; hi=(i+1)*n//N
          if hi>lo: out[i]=a[lo:hi].sum()
       # alternatively use np.interp with cumulative? simple loop N up to 2048, n maybe 512; 1M operations if many? only few. Use vectorized:
       idx=np.arange(n)
       # maybe use np.add.reduceat
   return normalize_nonneg(out)
```
Use `np.interp` for downsample? To preserve sum, map cumulative: cumulative integral. `np.interp(x_new, np.linspace(0,1,n), cumsum?)` not trivial. Loop okay: 2048*512? Actually for each i sum slice total n operations. Fine.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start=time.monotonic()
    total_budget=max(60.0,float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline=start + total_budget - 80.0
    state={'score':float('inf'),'seq':None}
    # provided
    prov=load_provided_sequence()
    if prov is not None:
        sc=score_fast(prov)
        update_global(state,prov,sc,'provided')
    # fallback
    fallback=np.array([1.0/3,2.0/3])
    sc=score_fast(fallback)
    update_global(state,fallback,sc,'fallback')
    # maybe uniform 512
    update_global(state,np.ones(512)/512, score_fast(np.ones(512)/512),'uniform512')

    N1=512
    if hard_deadline - time.monotonic() < 30:
       return final
    # generate pool 512
    pool=generate_pool(N1,rng)
    scored=[]
    for name,a in pool:
        sc=score_fast(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,name,a))
    scored.sort(key=tuple_score_key)
    print candidates top
    budget = max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic())
    # phase budgets
    b1 = min(900.0, budget*0.50)
    b2 = min(600.0, budget*0.30)
    b3 = min(250.0, budget*0.20)
    # If b1 + b2 + b3 > budget - 50: scale
    ssum=b1+b2+b3
    if ssum > budget - 50:
       factor=(budget-50)/ssum
       b1*=factor; b2*=factor; b3*=factor
    # Phase1
    if b1 > 10 and scored:
        # use top tasks
        props=[0.4,0.25,0.15,0.1,0.05,0.05]
        for i,item in enumerate(scored[:len(props)]):
            lim=b1*props[i]
            if lim < 3: continue
            if time.monotonic() + 5 >= hard_deadline: break
            sc,name,a=item
            local_search(a, lim, rng, state, f"p1_{name}_{i}", hard_deadline)
    # Phase2: 1024
    if state['seq'] is not None and b2 > 10 and time.monotonic()+10 < hard_deadline:
        a2=resize_to_length(state['seq'], 1024)
        # also generate direct pool at 1024? maybe if b2>300: score few top?
        local_search(a2, b2, rng, state, "p2_up_1024", hard_deadline)
    # Phase3
    if state['seq'] is not None and b3 > 10 and time.monotonic()+10 < hard_deadline:
        a3=resize_to_length(state['seq'], 2048)
        local_search(a3, b3, rng, state, "p3_up_2048", hard_deadline)
    # final polish at current best length if time left
    rem=hard_deadline-time.monotonic()
    if state['seq'] is not None and rem > 60:
        local_search(state['seq'], rem-30, rng, state, "final", hard_deadline)
    seq=state['seq']
    if seq is None: seq=np.array([1/3,2/3])
    cand=[float(x) for x in seq]
    try: official=evaluate_sequence(cand) ...
    except: pass
    return cand
```

Let's detail `generate_pool` with no lambdas.

```
def generate_pool(N, rng):
    pool=[]
    N=int(N)
    if N<2: N=2
    x=(np.arange(N)+0.5)/float(N)
    invN=1.0/N
    # base L
    L=np.ones_like(x)
    idx=x>0
    L[idx]=1.0/np.sqrt(x[idx])
    L=normalize_nonneg(L)
    add_pool(pool,'L_pure',L)
    # Leps
    for e in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.5,3.0):
        add_pool(pool,f'Leps_{e}', 1.0/(np.sqrt(x)+e))
    # powers
    for p in (0.0,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0,5.0):
        q=(p+1.0)*(1.0-x)**p
        add_pool(pool,f'q_{p}', q)
        rp=(p+1.0)*x**p
        add_pool(pool,f'inc_{p}', rp)
    # mix full support
    for p in (0.0,0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0):
        q=normalize_nonneg((p+1.0)*(1.0-x)**p)
        for w in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.15,0.2,0.3,0.5):
            add_pool(pool,f'mixLq_{p}_{w}', (1-w)*L + w*q)
        # with Leps 0.2
    Le=normalize_nonneg(1.0/(np.sqrt(x)+0.2))
    for p in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
        q=normalize_nonneg((p+1.0)*(1.0-x)**p)
        for w in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3):
            add_pool(pool,f'mixLeq_{p}_{w}', (1-w)*Le + w*q)
    # piecewise left
    for p in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5):
        mask=x<p
        Lm_raw=np.where(mask, 1.0/np.sqrt(x+1e-9), 0.0)
        Lm=normalize_nonneg(Lm_raw)
        U=normalize_nonneg(np.where(mask, 0.0, 1.0))
        for w in (0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9):
            add_pool(pool,f'pieceL_{p}_{w}', (1-w)*Lm + w*U)
        q=normalize_nonneg(np.where(mask, 0.0, (1.0-x)**2))
        for w in (0.1,0.25,0.5):
            add_pool(pool,f'pieceLq_{p}_{w}', (1-w)*Lm + w*q)
    # piecewise right with q? mirror
    for p in (0.1,0.2,0.4):
       mask=x>1-p
       Rm_raw=np.where(mask, 1.0/np.sqrt(1.0-x+1e-9), 0.0)
       Rm=normalize_nonneg(Rm_raw)
       U=normalize_nonneg(np.where(mask,0.0,1.0))
       for w in (0.1,0.3,0.5,0.7):
           add_pool(pool,f'pieceR_{p}_{w}', (1-w)*Rm + w*U)
    # random smooth
    for i in range(10):
       noise=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,size=N)
       wlen=max(2, min(N, N//20 or 2))
       if wlen>1:
          w=np.ones(wlen)/float(wlen)
          noise=np.convolve(noise,w,mode='same')
       add_pool(pool,f'rand_{i}', np.exp(0.6*noise))
    # exponentials
    for beta in (0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0,8.0):
        add_pool(pool,f'expm_{beta}', np.exp(-beta*x))
        add_pool(pool,f'expplus_{beta}', np.exp(beta*x))
    # sine moderate
    for freq in (2,3,5):
       add_pool(pool,f'sine_{freq}', 1.0 + 0.8*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*x))
       add_pool(pool,f'cosine_{freq}', 1.0 + 0.8*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x))
    return pool
```
Potential issue `wlen=max(2, min(N, N//20 or 2))`: if N<20, N//20=0, or 2 gives 2; max2. Good. But `min(N, 2)`. fine.

Pool size: not huge.

`score_pool`:
```
def score_pool(pool):
   scored=[]
   for name,a in pool:
      try:
        a=normalize_nonneg(a)
        sc=score_fast(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,name,a))
      except: pass
   scored.sort(key=tuple_score_key)
   return scored
```

`run_top_tasks(scored,total_time,rng,state,name_prefix,hard_deadline)` top-level to avoid duplicating.
```
def run_top_tasks(scored,total_time,rng,state,prefix,hard_deadline):
    if total_time <=5 or not scored: return
    props=[0.40,0.25,0.15,0.10,0.05,0.05]
    for i, item in enumerate(scored[:len(props)]):
       if i>=len(scored): break
       limit=total_time*props[i]
       if limit<2: continue
       if time.monotonic() + 2.0 >= hard_deadline: break
       sc,name,a=item
       local_search(a, limit, rng, state, f"{prefix}_{i}_{name}"[:50], hard_deadline)
```
Name with colon? Not issue. But `f"{prefix}_{i}_{name}"` could be long; print okay.

`phase_budget` calculation in propose.

Now `lp_move` line search. To avoid LP dominating, local_search calls only no_improve>=60, n<=1200, at most every 20s. Implementation:
```
def lp_move(a,c,M):
   if linprog is None: return None
   n=a.size
   if n<2 or M<=1e-12: return None
   if n<=300: max_rows=2*n-1
   elif n<=800: max_rows=min(2*n-1, 700)
   else: max_rows=min(2*n-1, 450)
   rows=select_active_rows(c,max_rows)
   A=...
   if not np.any(A): return None
   A=A/M
   cvec=-np.ones(n)
   try:
       res=linprog(cvec,A_ub=A,b_ub=np.ones(A.shape[0]),bounds=(0.0,None),method='highs')
   except: return None
   if res.status==0 and res.x is not None:
       g=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
       g=np.clip(g,0,None)
       s=float(g.sum())
       if s>1e-12: return g/s
   return None
```
Maybe `linprog` high-level with large dense A and no bounds upper could be slow; but limited.
Could add `options={'maxiter':1000}`? highs has options. Not necessary.

def _mix_score(theta, N):
    w=float(np.clip(theta[0],0,1)); p=float(np.clip(theta[1],0,5))
    x=(np.arange(N)+0.5)/N
    L=normalize_nonneg(1/np.sqrt(x))
    q=normalize_nonneg((p+1)*(1-x)**p)
    a=(1-w)*L + w*q
    return score_fast(a)
```

Top-level:
```
def make_Lq_sequence(theta,N):
    w=float(np.clip(theta[0],0.0,1.0)); p=float(np.clip(theta[1],0.0,6.0))
    x=(np.arange(N)+0.5)/float(N)
    L=normalize_nonneg(1.0/np.sqrt(x))
    q=normalize_nonneg((p+1.0)*(1.0-x)**p)
    return normalize_nonneg((1.0-w)*L + w*q)

def mix_Lq_score(theta,N):
    a=make_Lq_sequence(theta,N)
    return score_fast(a)
```

Numpy `fft` DFT X_m=∑_n x_n e^{-2πi nm/N}. `ifft(X_m Y_m)_k = 1/N ∑_m X_m Y_m e^{2πi mk/N}`. If Y_m=conj(A_m)=∑_j a_j e^{2πi jm/N}. Then X_m Y_m = ∑_l p_l e^{-2πi lm/N} ∑_j a_j e^{2πi jm/N}. ```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / sum(a)^2

The program combines:
  - many analytic initial guesses (singular left-sided laws, regularized
    inverse-square-root laws, mixture families, piecewise laws, smoothed randoms),
  - a coarse low-dimensional Nelder-Mead optimization for a useful mixture family,
  - a local first-order subgradient / softmax-soft-max optimizer on the simplex,
  - occasional active-set LP escape directions,
  - graduated length upsampling (512 -> 1024 -> 2048).

All returned values are non-negative and finite.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    """Clip to non-negative, replace non-finite values by zero, normalize sum to 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        n = max(1, a.size)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def convolution(a):
    """Linear convolution a*a.  Direct for small n, FFT otherwise."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 256:
        return np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:need]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def score_fast(a):
    """Exact internal score for non-negative sequence, normalized or not."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    c = convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def corr_pa(p, a):
    """
    r[i] = sum_j a[j] p[j+i] for i=0..len(a)-1.
    This is the gradient (without factor 2) of p-dot(a*a).
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or p.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    r = np.fft.irfft(fp * np.conj(fa), size)[:n]
    r = np.asarray(r, dtype=np.float64)
    r[r < 0.0] = 0.0
    return r


def softmax_lag_weights(c, sharp):
    """Softmax over convolution coefficients, scaled by sharpness sharp."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.ones_like(c) / float(c.size)
    z = float(sharp) * (c - m) / m
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.zeros_like(c)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p
    return p / s


def try_step(a, p, current_score, eta_list):
    """
    Multiplicative simplex step using softmax subgradient.
    Returns (best_h, best_score) or (None, current_score).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None, current_score
    r = corr_pa(p, a)
    r = np.maximum(r, 0.0)
    mean_r = float(np.dot(a, r))
    s = r - mean_r
    scale = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(s * s)))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-15:
        return None, current_score
    s = s / scale

    # tiny floor: allows recovering zero coordinates without changing scale
    base = a + 1e-12
    bs = float(base.sum())
    if bs <= 0.0:
        return None, current_score
    base = base / bs

    best_h = None
    best_sc = float(current_score)

    for eta in eta_list:
        logw = -float(eta) * s
        # Remove weighted mean in log-space (does not change normalized h, improves stability)
        logw = logw - float(np.dot(logw, a))
        logw = np.clip(logw, -6.0, 6.0)
        h = base * np.exp(logw)
        hs = float(h.sum())
        if hs <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(hs):
            continue
        h = h / hs
        sc = score_fast(h)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_h = h
    return best_h, best_sc


def random_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.1):
    """Smooth multiplicative perturbation with small floor to avoid sticking at zero."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    log_noise = rng.normal(0.0, float(strength), size=n)
    wlen = max(2, min(n, 1 + max(1, n // 100)))
    if wlen > 1:
        w = np.ones(wlen, dtype=np.float64) / float(wlen)
        log_noise = np.convolve(log_noise, w, mode="same")
    h = a + 1e-12
    h = h * np.exp(log_noise)
    if n > 6 and rng.random() < 0.25:
        width = max(2, min(n // 5, n))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        h[start:start + width] *= np.exp(float(strength) * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0))
    return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(h, 0.0))


def select_active_rows(c, max_rows):
    """Select active convolution lags for LP: top lags plus a global spread."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = c.size
    if m <= 1 or max_rows >= m:
        return np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    k = int(min(max_rows, m))
    top = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:].astype(np.int64)
    # Add evenly spaced rows to avoid unbounded LP directions for endpoint variables.
    fill_nt = min(40, max(6, m // 64))
    fill = np.linspace(0, m - 1, fill_nt).astype(np.int64)
    rows = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, fill)))
    if rows.size > max_rows + 80:
        # Keep the requested number of top rows plus a smaller fill.
        small_fill = np.linspace(0, m - 1, max(6, fill_nt // 2)).astype(np.int64)
        rows = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, small_fill)))
    return rows


def lp_move(a, c, M):
    """
    LP escape direction:
        maximize sum(g) s.t. (a*g)[k] <= M for active k, g >= 0.
    Returns normalized g or None.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or M <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None
    if n <= 300:
        max_rows = 2 * n - 1
    elif n <= 800:
        max_rows = min(2 * n - 1, 700)
    elif n <= 1600:
        max_rows = min(2 * n - 1, 500)
    else:
        return None

    rows = select_active_rows(c, max_rows)
    if rows.size == 0:
        return None

    A = np.zeros((rows.size, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r, kk in enumerate(rows):
        kk = int(kk)
        jlo = max(0, kk - n + 1)
        jhi = min(n, kk + 1)
        if jhi > jlo:
            idx = jlo + np.arange(jhi - jlo, dtype=np.int64)
            A[r, idx] = a[kk - idx]

    if not np.any(A):
        return None

    A = A / M
    cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bvec = np.ones(A.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        res = linprog(
            cvec,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=bvec,
            bounds=(0.0, 100.0),
            method="highs",
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is not None and getattr(res, "status", 1) == 0 and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
        s = float(g.sum())
        if s > 1e-12:
            return g / s
    return None


def update_global(state, seq, score, tag=""):
    """Update global best in state dict."""
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return False
    seq = normalize_nonneg(seq)
    if seq.size == 0:
        return False
    old = state["score"]
    if score < old - 1e-12:
        state["score"] = float(score)
        state["seq"] = seq.copy()
        if not np.isfinite(old) or score < old - 1e-4:
            print(f"update {tag} score={score:.12f} n={seq.size}", flush=True)
        return True
    return False


def tuple_score_key(item):
    """Sort key for (score, name, array) tuples."""
    return item[0]


def add_pool(pool, name, a):
    """Add a normalized non-negative sequence to a candidate pool."""
    try:
        a = normalize_nonneg(a)
        if a.size >= 2:
            pool.append((name, a))
    except Exception:
        pass


def generate_pool(N, rng):
    """Generate a diverse set of initial non-negative sequences of length N."""
    pool = []
    N = int(max(2, N))
    x = (np.arange(N) + 0.5) / float(N)

    # Pure singular left-sided law
    L_raw = 1.0 / np.sqrt(x + 1e-12)
    L = normalize_nonneg(L_raw)
    add_pool(pool, "L_pure", L)

    # Regularized inverse-square-root laws
    for e in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40, 0.80, 1.50, 3.00):
        add_pool(pool, f"Leps_{e:.2f}", 1.0 / (np.sqrt(x) + e))

    # Decreasing power laws (vanish at 1 for p > 0)
    for p in (0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0):
        q = (p + 1.0) * (1.0 - x) ** p
        add_pool(pool, f"qpow_{p:.2f}", q)
        rp = (p + 1.0) * x ** p
        add_pool(pool, f"rpow_{p:.2f}", rp)

    # Mixtures of left singular law and right-vanishing powers
    Le = normalize_nonneg(1.0 / (np.sqrt(x) + 0.2))
    for p in (0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        q = normalize_nonneg((p + 1.0) * (1.0 - x) ** p)
        for w in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50):
            add_pool(pool, f"mixLq_{p:.1f}_{w:.2f}", (1.0 - w) * L + w * q)
            if p >= 0.5:
                add_pool(pool, f"mixLeq_{p:.1f}_{w:.2f}", (1.0 - w) * Le + w * q)

    # Piecewise left singular + flat/power tail
    for p in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50):
        mask = x < p
        Lm = normalize_nonneg(np.where(mask, 1.0 / np.sqrt(x + 1e-9), 0.0))
        U = normalize_nonneg(np.where(mask, 0.0, 1.0))
        for w in (0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50, 0.70, 0.90):
            add_pool(pool, f"pieceL_{p:.2f}_{w:.2f}", (1.0 - w) * Lm + w * U)
        q = normalize_nonneg(np.where(mask, 0.0, (1.0 - x) ** 2))
        for w in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50):
            add_pool(pool, f"pieceLq_{p:.2f}_{w:.2f}", (1.0 - w) * Lm + w * q)

    # Piecewise right singular starts (useful as basins in local search)
    for p in (0.10, 0.20, 0.40):
        mask = x > 1.0 - p
        Rm = normalize_nonneg(np.where(mask, 1.0 / np.sqrt((1.0 - x) + 1e-9), 0.0))
        U = normalize_nonneg(np.where(mask, 0.0, 1.0))
        for w in (0.10, 0.30, 0.50, 0.70):
            add_pool(pool, f"pieceR_{p:.2f}_{w:.2f}", (1.0 - w) * Rm + w * U)

    # Exponentials
    for beta in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        add_pool(pool, f"expm_{beta:.1f}", np.exp(-beta * x))
        add_pool(pool, f"expplus_{beta:.1f}", np.exp(beta * x))

    # Low-frequency waves
    for freq in (2, 3, 5):
        add_pool(pool, f"sine_{freq}", 1.0 + 0.8 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * x))
        add_pool(pool, f"cosine_{freq}", 1.0 + 0.8 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * x))

    # Smooth randoms
    for i in range(12):
        noise = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=N)
        wlen = max(2, min(N, max(2, N // 25)))
        if wlen > 1:
            w = np.ones(wlen, dtype=np.float64) / float(wlen)
            noise = np.convolve(noise, w, mode="same")
        add_pool(pool, f"rand_{i}", np.exp(0.55 * noise))

    return pool


def score_pool(pool):
    """Score and sort a pool of (name, array) candidates."""
    scored = []
    for name, a in pool:
        try:
            a = normalize_nonneg(a)
            sc = score_fast(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, name, a))
        except Exception:
            pass
    scored.sort(key=tuple_score_key)
    return scored


def resize_to_length(a, N):
    """Resize a normalized sequence to length N by interpolation or bin-summing."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    N = int(max(2, N))
    if n == N:
        return a
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
    if n < N:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N)
        out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
        return normalize_nonneg(out)
    # Downsample: sum bins, preserving mass
    out = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(N):
        lo = (i * n) // N
        hi = ((i + 1) * n) // N
        if hi > lo:
            out[i] = a[lo:hi].sum()
    return normalize_nonneg(out)


def load_provided_sequence():
    """Load previously provided construction if available."""
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size < 2 or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s


def local_search(a, time_limit, rng, state, name="", hard_deadline=None):
    """
    Long-running local optimizer for one sequence.
    Uses softmax subgradient steps, top-lag subgradients, random restarts,
    and occasional LP escape moves.
    """
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return a

    now = time.monotonic()
    deadline = now + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    if hard_deadline is not None:
        deadline = min(deadline, float(hard_deadline))

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score_fast(a)
    update_global(state, best, best_score, f"start {name}")

    no_improve = 0
    sharp = 10.0
    last_lp = 0.0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1

        c = convolution(a)
        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        cur = float(2.0 * n * M)

        if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = cur
            update_global(state, best, best_score, f"improve {name} it={it}")
            no_improve = 0
            # When improving, keep the soft-max moderately broad.
            sharp = max(4.0, sharp * 0.99)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 8:
                sharp = min(80.0, sharp * 1.03)

        # Periodic restart / anneal
        if no_improve >= 160:
            t = float(rng.uniform(0.4, 1.0))
            pert = random_perturb(best, rng, 0.25)
            a = normalize_nonneg(t * best + (1.0 - t) * pert)
            no_improve = 0
            sharp = float(rng.uniform(5.0, 40.0))
            continue

        # Choose subgradient distribution over lags
        if rng.random() < 0.22:
            k = int(rng.integers(1, 7))  # top 1..6 lags
            if k >= c.size:
                idx = np.arange(c.size)
            else:
                idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
            p = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
            if idx.size:
                p[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        elif rng.random() < 0.18:
            # broader mixture to flatten plateaus
            p = softmax_lag_weights(c, max(2.0, sharp * 0.25))
        else:
            p = softmax_lag_weights(c, sharp)

        if no_improve > 25:
            eta_list = (0.35, 1.0, 3.0, 6.0)
        else:
            eta_list = (0.25, 0.8, 2.5)

        new_a, new_sc = try_step(a, p, cur, eta_list)
        if new_a is not None:
            a = new_a
            cur = new_sc
            if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = cur
                update_global(state, best, best_score, f"step {name} it={it}")
                no_improve = 0
                continue

        # If flat for a while, try an LP escape.
        if (
            no_improve >= 45
            and n <= 1200
            and (time.monotonic() - last_lp) > 12.0
        ):
            last_lp = time.monotonic()
            g = lp_move(a, c, M)
            if g is not None:
                for tt in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.55, 0.80, 1.0):
                    h = normalize_nonneg((1.0 - tt) * a + tt * g)
                    sc = score_fast(h)
                    if sc < cur:
                        a = h
                        cur = sc
                        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                            best = a.copy()
                            best_score = sc
                            update_global(state, best, best_score, f"lp {name} it={it}")
                            no_improve = 0
                        break

        # Very tiny random nudge so the trajectory does not freeze
        if no_improve % 9 == 0:
            a = random_perturb(a, rng, 0.04)

    # Final evaluation of the active trajectory
    try:
        final_sc = score_fast(a)
        update_global(state, a, final_sc, f"end {name}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best


def run_top_tasks(scored, total_time, rng, state, prefix, hard_deadline):
    """Run local search on top pool candidates with graded time budgets."""
    if scored is None or len(scored) == 0 or total_time <= 5.0:
        return
    props = (0.40, 0.25, 0.15, 0.10, 0.05, 0.05)
    for i, item in enumerate(scored[:len(props)]):
        limit = float(total_time) * float(props[i])
        if limit < 3.0:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() + 2.0 >= hard_deadline:
            break
        sc, name, a = item
        local_search(a, limit, rng, state, f"{prefix}_{i}_{name}"[:80], hard_deadline)


def make_Lq_sequence(theta, N, x):
    """
    Two-parameter family:
        f = (1 - w) * normalize(1/sqrt(x)) + w * normalize((p+1)*(1-x)^p)
    with 0 <= w <= 1 and 0 <= p <= 6.
    """
    if x is None or x.size != N:
        x = (np.arange(N) + 0.5) / float(N)
    w = float(np.clip(theta[0], 0.0, 1.0))
    p = float(np.clip(theta[1], 0.0, 6.0))
    L = normalize_nonneg(1.0 / np.sqrt(x + 1e-12))
    q = normalize_nonneg((p + 1.0) * (1.0 - x) ** p)
    return normalize_nonneg((1.0 - w) * L + w * q)


def mix_Lq_score(theta, N, x):
    """Score for make_Lq_sequence; used by Nelder-Mead."""
    try:
        a = make_Lq_sequence(theta, N, x)
        return score_fast(a)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.  Returns a list of non-negative floats.
    Uses up to budget_s seconds, reserving time for final CSV/independent checks.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = t_start + (total_budget - 80.0)

    state = {"score": float("inf"), "seq": None}

    # Provided construction, if any
    prov = load_provided_sequence()
    if prov is not None:
        if prov.size > 4096:
            prov = resize_to_length(prov, 4096)
        sc = score_fast(prov)
        update_global(state, prov, sc, "provided")
        if 512 <= prov.size <= 2048:
            pass  # will also be considered in scored starts if added below

    # Guaranteed fallback
    fallback = np.array([1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0], dtype=np.float64)
    update_global(state, fallback, score_fast(fallback), "fallback")

    # Primary length
    N1 = 512
    if time.monotonic() + 20.0 >= hard_deadline:
        return [float(x) for x in state["seq"]] if state["seq"] is not None else [
            float(1.0 / 3.0), float(2.0 / 3.0)
        ]

    # Build initial starts at 512
    starts = []
    if prov is not None:
        starts.append(("provided", prov))
        starts.append(("provided_512", resize_to_length(prov, 512)))

    pool = generate_pool(N1, rng)
    starts.extend(pool)

    # Quick low-dimensional optimization for one useful mixture family
    if minimize is not None and time.monotonic() + 30.0 < hard_deadline:
        x1 = (np.arange(N1) + 0.5) / float(N1)
        for x0 in (
            (0.04, 0.5),
            (0.06, 1.0),
            (0.10, 1.5),
            (0.18, 2.0),
            (0.30, 0.8),
        ):
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    mix_Lq_score,
                    np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64),
                    args=(N1, x1),
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={"maxiter": 350, "xatol": 0.02, "fatol": 1e-6},
                )
                if res is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun):
                    add_pool(starts, f"Lq_nm_{x0[0]}_{x0[1]}", make_Lq_sequence(res.x, N1, x1))
            except Exception:
                pass

    scored = score_pool(starts)
    if scored:
        print(
            "top512 initial: "
            + ", ".join(f"{sc:.8f}" for sc, _, _ in scored[:5]),
            flush=True,
        )

    # Phase budgets
    budget = max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic())
    b1 = min(900.0, budget * 0.50)
    b2 = min(650.0, budget * 0.30)
    b3 = min(250.0, budget * 0.20)
    ss = b1 + b2 + b3
    if ss > max(1.0, budget - 50.0):
        factor = max(0.0, (budget - 50.0)) / max(1.0, ss)
        b1 *= factor
        b2 *= factor
        b3 *= factor

    # Phase 1: optimize at length 512
    if b1 > 5.0 and scored:
        run_top_tasks(scored, b1, rng, state, "p512", hard_deadline)

    # Phase 2: length 1024 from best-so-far and direct 1024 pool
    if b2 > 10.0 and time.monotonic() + 20.0 < hard_deadline:
        cand2 = []
        if state["seq"] is not None:
            a_up = resize_to_length(state["seq"], 1024)
            sc_up = score_fast(a_up)
            if np.isfinite(sc_up):
                cand2.append((sc_up, "up512_1024", a_up))
        if b2 > 40.0 and time.monotonic() + 30.0 < hard_deadline:
            pool2 = generate_pool(1024, rng)
            scored2 = score_pool(pool2)
            for item in scored2[:4]:
                cand2.append(item)
        if cand2:
            cand2.sort(key=tuple_score_key)
            a2 = cand2[0][2]
            local_search(a2, b2, rng, state, "p1024", hard_deadline)

    # Phase 3: length 2048 from best-so-far
    if b3 > 10.0 and state["seq"] is not None and time.monotonic() + 20.0 < hard_deadline:
        a3 = resize_to_length(state["seq"], 2048)
        local_search(a3, b3, rng, state, "p2048", hard_deadline)

    # Final polish at current best length, if time remains
    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if state["seq"] is not None and rem > 60.0:
        local_search(state["seq"], rem - 30.0, rng, state, "final", hard_deadline)

    seq = state["seq"]
    if seq is None:
        seq = np.array([1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0], dtype=np.float64)

    # Cap final length for safe independent evaluation time
    if seq.size > 4096:
        seq = resize_to_length(seq, 4096)

    cand = [float(x) for x in normalize_nonneg(seq)]

    try:
        official = evaluate_sequence(cand)  # noqa: F821
        if np.isfinite(official):
            print(f"official_best score={official:.12f} n={len(cand)}", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"official_invalid n={len(cand)}", flush=True)
    except Exception as ex:
        print(f"official_eval_error {ex}", flush=True)

    return cand
```
